# RH frontier: active routes and route candidates

Sources: docs/reports/endpoints.md (259 families: 205 LIVE, 54 NO-GO); docs/reports/STATUS.md; local Lean corpus; route cards. Killed mechanisms: docs/reports/rh-killed-routes.md. [L] = cited Lean declaration; [P] = paper proof; no fresh compilation implied.

**State.** RH open. 16 active routes; 23 candidates. Route proposals 1–3: stated mechanisms refuted, residuals live (KR-R1 → TG fallback, KR-R2 → RN, KR-R3 → WD). Active routes have RH-strength receivers, no source producer (STATUS F01, CANNOT-24, CANNOT-192r).

**Terms.** Receiver = proved implication/equivalence ending in RH whose premise is open. Producer = proof of such a premise from source data. Route = a receiver plus a named producer mechanism that is not excluded, plus one exact open step. Candidate = interface or fragment without a handoff to a receiver or without an independent open step. Killed = every proposed producer of the route is impossible, circular (premise = RH) or nonselective (see the killed ledger). "Active" does not mean promising.

**Codes.** F#### = endpoint family (endpoints.md). MUST-/CANNOT-/F-n/FAIL-n = STATUS.md entries (suffix `r` = reused number). KR-x, no-go-NNN = killed ledger entries. R:X = formal/GaloisForLFunctions/RHTower/X.lean; H:X = formal/GaloisForLFunctions/HTGeometry/X.lean; Pn:x = docs/drafts/pipeline/{0-backlog,1-in-progress,2-fully-proven,3-formalized,4-publication-ready,5-failed}[n]/x.md; ULF:x = P1:universal-law-field/x. Tags: [L] Lean, [P] paper proof only, [N] numerical only.

**Legacy labels → routes.** C1→RW · CCM-FU→FU · CLG/TTF, K-RVM, S6F, TCD, UEB→TH · CSB, STC, U, MPLF, RHist, BPC, TQFQ, WS2, SQB→RN · D2, Q, RG, RG-ETA, TK→PA · E, EDQ, L, HDL, PSRC→MC · FZZ-P, PW-B→PM · G, HC→WD · LI→LI · O→WL + RW (genus part killed: KR-GEN) · P, SP-E→RP · SV2, RG-HF→SZ · T1, T2, Route T→TT · Route T successor / ULF direct sub-unit anchor→TG · Deninger lanes→HF. Retired: HB, AKC, TMD (killed ledger).

## Gates every route must pass

Distilled from STATUS; each is a proved obstruction, not a preference.
- No slack. RH sits exactly on its boundary (de Bruijn–Newman `Λ=0`; lag ceiling `a_1` approached along the powers of every base). A proof is an exact identity or a rigidity, never an inequality with margin; log-lossy estimates are worthless (CANNOT-411, MUST-239/240, CANNOT-437).
- Exact primes. If `Λ+w` is Weil-positive and `Σ_{k≤n}w(k)=κ₁(n−1)+κ₂(√n−1)+E(n)`, `Σ|E(n)|/(n√n)<∞`, then `w=0` (MUST-507). In particular, nonzero `ℓ¹(n⁻¹ᐟ²)` or partial-sum `O(n^θ)`, `θ<½`, perturbations fail (MUST-502/503); weights supported on `O(N^θ)` positions, `θ<½`, fail. Sieve majorants, sparse weights and regular residue/prime-power shifts cannot replace exact weights. Full-cone `lam−Λ=o(Λ)` also forces lam=Λ [L], including bounded prime-only deviations (R:WeilLittleOVonMangoldtRigidity).
- Rigidity, not estimate. Any inequality whose hypotheses survive a single-prime Euler-factor deformation gives neither RH nor a zero-free region nor an off-line count (MUST-389; CANNOT-64/65).
- Orientation. The separating datum is reflection-odd and two-torsion; reflection-even real readers, localized/equivariant invariants and averages are blind to it (CANNOT-399, CANNOT-405/407, CANNOT-425, MUST-433).
- Individual zeros. Unconditional arithmetic identities see only pair-even smoothed densities; no fixed functional individuates a zero (CANNOT-182r).
- Exponent, not constant. The missing step moves an exponent by ½ at once; no method gives any exponent below ½ in the lag/Weil lanes (MUST-250, MUST-261).
- Cofinal source law. Finite data, fixed jets and finite packets are nonselective; the law must be proved on pre-divisor source data before specializing at Xi zeros (CANNOT-02, MUST-01, MUST-29/30). Any finite holomorphic decaying test family with an invertible line-pair derivative matrix admits a distant off-line quadruple plus support slides preserving every test value [L]; regular-tuple hypothesis retained (R:CriticalLocusRegularTupleBlindness).
- Mellin-diagonal inputs are blind. Dilations, dyadic/prime-2 self-similarity and the Hecke semigroup act on Mellin transforms by units or characters; common eigenvectors include Helson series with off-line zeros; together with the functional equation they pin ζ and leave no deformation handle (KR-R2).
- Holomorphic readers are unquantized. Zero-free anchored holomorphic approximants with bounded boundary argument have zero-free limits [L] (R:AnchoredPhasePassageRigidity); a holomorphic clock unimodular on the line gives one growing mode per free orbit (CANNOT-458). Source realizations of zero-side quantized forms are 0/∞ growth criteria: sub-unit margins are idle, any finite bound is RH; the open core is finiteness at the `√X` scale (KR-R1, KR-R3; CANNOT-461).

## Active routes

### WL — Weil lag function (legacy O)
Target: RH ⇔ `Φ(L)=Σ'_ρ e^{L(ρ−½)}/(ρ(1−ρ))` bounded; one side suffices; sharp form `a_n=Φ(log n) ≤ a_1=2+γ−log 4π` ∀n [L]. F0130.
Proved [L]: either one-sided bound `±D(T)≤B e^{T/2}` for `D(T)=∫₀ᵀ(e^t−ψ(e^t))dt` forces RH. E: R:ChebyshevDeficitPrimitiveOneSidedRH.
Proved [L]: dichotomy — all `a_n ∈ [−a_1,a_1]` or `(a_n)` unbounded both ways; `|Φ(L)| ≤ Ce^{L/2}e^{−cL^{1/10}}` unconditionally; `Φ ≤ Me^{aL}` ⇒ every `Re ρ ≤ ½+a`; bounded prime lags ⇒ `¼ ≤ Re ρ ≤ ¾` (Hoheisel); `Re G ≥ 0` on `Re s>½` unconditionally (reaches only `Re ρ ≤ 1`); Frobenius-orbit form `B(v_x,v_y)=Φ(log x−log y)`, RH ⇔ `B ≥ 0` on that span; Gaussian Euler–continuum passage: bounded `L¹` boundary argument of `Lₓ+Aₓ` on circles `C(1+iγ,r)`, `0<r<½`, along one sequence `x_k→0⁺` ⇒ RH (R:AnchoredPhasePassageRigidity); RH ⇔ holomorphic two-prime lag excess bounded ∀T (R:XiHolomorphicTwoPrimePencilGrowthDichotomy).
Open: one one-sided prime inequality at scale `√X`: `√X∫_X^∞(ψ(t)−t+log 2π+½log(1−t⁻²))t⁻²dt ≥ −C` (or the reverse), i.e. `∫_1^p m(t)dt ≤ C√p` at primes. Named producer shape: an archimedean Hodge index — intersection pairing on principal divisors of Spec ℤ with regularized values `Φ(log(n/m))` and Hodge inequality `Φ(0) ≥ Φ(L)` (MUST-229/231/236); residuals of routes 1 and 3 land here (KR-R1, KR-R3).
Proved [L, unconditional]: for `2≤n≤k≤m`, `a_k≤max(0,a_n,a_m)+(m−n)²/(8n√k)`; the alternative `(m−n)²/(4k√k)` bound also holds. Positive actual-prime ramps and convexity of the full trivial-zero correction give upper interpolation across every arithmetic interval. Any integer set bracketing each `k≥2` by `n,m` with `(m−n)²≤C n√n` is RH-complete for bounded upper lags, with global ceiling `max(M,0)+C/8`; arbitrary `n^{3/4}` meshes need no counting-series continuation or attained zero abscissa. E: R:WeilLagQuadraticInterpolation (`integerLag_curvature_upper_interpolation`, `quadraticLagMesh_characterization`).

Proved [L, unconditional]: exact scaled lag chord deficit is logarithmic defect `J` minus nonnegative triangular prime charge `Q` minus nonnegative trivial-zero chord deficit. Wider meshes carry the same bounded-upper-lag RH criterion when their explicit arithmetic defect `J−Q≤C√k`; every quadratic mesh meets this condition. This retains the prime payment lost by geometric interpolation. A uniform payment certificate or a bound on sample lags remains open; no positivity producer or optimality claim. E: R:WeilLagQuadraticInterpolation (`sqrt_integerLag_chord_exact`, `primeCompensatedLagMesh_characterization`, `quadraticLagMesh_implies_primeCompensated`).
Proved [L, unconditional]: for `2≤n≤k≤m`, `u,v≥0`, `u+v=1`, `k=un+vm`, `J−Q=un∫_n^k(ψ(t)−t−c)t⁻²dt−vm∫_k^m(ψ(t)−t−c)t⁻²dt` for every constant `c`. Far tail and constant level cancel; the actual interior lag has an upper certificate from endpoint lags plus this signed local work divided by `√k`. Open: signed local-work bound `≤C√k` on a chosen mesh; upper sample-lag bound. E: R:WeilLagLocalChebyshevChord (`lagPrimeChordDefect_eq_centered_local_chebyshevWork`, `integerLag_local_chebyshev_upper_interpolation`); P1:wl-local-chebyshev-chord-work-2026-09-29.
Proved [L]: actual primes form a quadratic integer-lag mesh via unconditional 71/100 gaps, with a fixed prime covering the initial segment. Bounded upper prime lags ⇔ RH, sharp ceiling a₁; one-sided prime growth exponent sharp. Mesh geometry supplies no sample-lag ceiling. E: R:WeilLagPrimeQuadraticMesh; R:PrimeLagLandauPrimesOnly; R:PrimeLagGrowthExponent.
Proved [L, unconditional]: for every finite probability mean on integer nodes `n≥r≥2` with integer barycenter `k`, `√k a_k≤Σ w_n√n a_n+Σ w_n(n−k)²/(2r)`. Fixed geometric mean schedules with variance `≤C r√k` are RH-complete for bounded upper normalized signed means, with global ceiling `M+C/2`. This permits negative lag values to contribute to the receiver; no individual sample ceiling is required. Open: a source proof bounding the actual signed means on one such schedule. E: R:WeilLagBarycentricMean (`sqrt_integerLag_le_finite_mean_add_variance`, `barycentricLagMeanScheme_characterization`); P1:wl-barycentric-signed-lag-mean-2026-09-29.
Proved [L, unconditional]: if `k≥4`, `2h≤k`, `M,δ≥0`, `h²≤δ k√k` and `a_k>M+δ`, then every lag on `[k−h,k]` exceeds `M`, or every lag on `[k,k+h]` exceeds `M`. Thus a central upper excursion forces an entire high-lag side with a radius depending on height; `δ=1` permits the `k^(3/4)` scale. Open: combine this with actual short-window prime mass to quantify high-prime populations; prove an opposite low-lag population producer. E: R:WeilLagExcursionBlocks (`integerLag_upper_excursion_has_height_scaled_block`, `integerLag_upper_excursion_has_one_sided_block`); P1:wl-upper-lag-excursion-block-2026-09-29.
Closed around it: uniform-gap producers and finite prime truncations (MUST-234/245); de la Vallée Poussin positivity (CANNOT-341); one base orbit or sparse sampling support (CANNOT-327/328); self-pairings (CANNOT-325); lag Gram/sign shortcuts (no-go-149, 441); genus fixed part (KR-GEN).
E: R:WeilLagFunctionInteger; R:WeilLagFunctionRecurrence; R:WeilLagFunctionOmega; R:PrimeLagLandauSeries; R:LagPairThresholdSharp; P1:integer-lag-law-notes; R:AnchoredPhasePassageRigidity; R:XiHolomorphicTwoPrimePencilGrowthDichotomy.


### RW — Restricted Weil ground energy (legacy C1, O, CCM wall)
Target: RH ⇔ constrained ground energy `μ₀(B) ≥ 0` at every aperture (Bombieri–Yoshida) [L]. F0038, F0034, F0031, F0023, F0018, F0007, F0039, F0066, F0101, F0173, F0274, F0080, F0016, F0037, F0035.
Proved [L]: even-pole energy nonnegative at every aperture ⇔ RH; odd-pole energy alone has the same equivalence. A weight system majorizing `Λ` with all-aperture even-pole positivity forces RH. E: R:WeilParityPoleOneSidedRH.
Proved [L]: `μ₀` strictly antitone, at most one zero aperture (positive below, negative above); RH ⇔ `L¹`-normalized Weil negativity sub-exponential in aperture; hinge sum `H(t) ≤ M(t)+Ce^{εt}` ⇔ every `|Re ρ−½| ≤ ε`; RH ⇔ `|∫_0^T(e^t−ψ(e^t))dt| ≤ Ke^{T/2}`; all Weil negativity is a conjugation defect; failure of RH yields a first-contact exact-kernel state irreducible under translation from every narrower aperture (`riemannHypothesis_or_ccmFirstContactTranslationSaturation`); lower prime–Gamma Cauchy atoms approximate all Weil test vectors; pole-vanishing ground energy `μ₀(½ log 2) ≥ 1/10` by a computer-free trace certificate `μ₀ ≥ α − ∫(α−W)₊ dist²(e_ξ, span e^{±x/2})`, and `μ₀ ≥ 1/20` with the prime 2 for all apertures up to `λ₁ = √2·e^{1/100}` via the free lag-`2b` term (Krein extension) (R:PoleVanishingWeilPositivityHalfLogTwo, R:PoleVanishingWeilPositivityPrimeTwo; computer-free; certified-computation positivity with primes on support `[−0.8, 0.8]` is Zhu, arXiv 2608.24827).
Proved [L]: uncompressed finite-prime transport `T_a` on `L²(−a,a)` has `‖T_a‖=(1+o(1))e^a`; pole-profile weighted Schur row = Chebyshev `ψ` + reciprocal-Mangoldt sum; elementary bound `‖T_a‖≤(log 4)e^a+4ae^{a/2}+2a+log 4+4`. Pole-compressed prime translation still has `‖P_{V₀}T_aP_{V₀}‖≥e^a/(64a)` for large `a`, unconditionally: high-frequency Dirichlet resonance survives exact pole correction (CANNOT-474). Its norm exponent is 1, height-blind; RH content is archimedean-relative Weil-form control, not subexponential operator norm. E: R:PoleFreePrimeTranslationResonance.
Proved [L]: exact actual-prime Schur edge `E_a(f)≥0` satisfies `E_a(f)+Re⟨T_af,f⟩=rowPotential_a(f)` and, for every `ε>0` at large `a`, `E_a(f)+Re⟨T_af,f⟩≤(1+ε)e^a‖f‖²`; the full source lower bound retains `+E_a` and signed polar terms. On exactly pole-constrained form-domain vectors at every fixed aperture, nonzero-mass weakly null sequences have `E_a→0`: no edge-only positive physical-norm gap. Their complete source escapes positively through Gamma; edge-null does not imply a negative Weil witness. Actual low/high outer prime bands have uniform normalized cross-alignment deficit, but this does not lower-bound native Xi energy. Supported coherent projection retains all cross terms and only discards nonnegative invisible cost. E: R:CCMPrimePoleSchurSharpDefect; R:CCMPrimePoleSchurConstrainedEscape; R:CCMPrimePoleSchurGammaEscape; R:CCMActualPrimeUniformBandSaving; R:CCMActualHeadSupportedCoherentGamma.
Proved [L]: if `2a≥log 2+log 3`, `E_a(f)=0` on physical `L²` forces `f∈ℂ·(e^{x/2}+e^{-x/2})`; exact pole constraints remove this kernel. On genuine constrained form-domain vectors, `‖Ju‖²≤ε‖u‖²_form+C(a,ε)E_a(Ju)` for every `ε>0`; `C(a,ε)` has no cofinal rate. Supported coherent Γ cost is positive on the pole vector yet is not bounded by `C E_a` at any fixed `2a>log 2`. ### RP — Route-P / SP-E history floor (legacy P, SP-E)
Target: RH ⇔ eventual linear floor of the physical Route-P normalized-variance history, `∃B ∀K≥3: defect(K) ≥ −B(K+2)` [L]. F0188, F0187, F0120, F0086.

Proved [L, source-domain obstruction]: exact binomial packets at one prime tend to zero in every polynomial radial graph energy and in the complete Xi jet Hilbert norm while retaining their original-depth coefficient. Positivity of the undamped packet derivative supplies a vanishing real Laplace budget times a Cauchy factor, uniformly on the full Xi strip. Rescaled even packets have Xi limit zero and a strictly positive literal matched parabolic second-moment limit; the source-pair relation has a nonzero vertical closure vector, excluding even closed single-valued partial recovery of that moment in this topology. The explicit boundary datum survives; the linear floor for the complete matched-minus-quantile signed history remains open.
Open: a source-derived indefinite linear-floor/payment law for centroid and square debts, retaining the unbounded ratio-one terminal excursions, plus a coefficient-faithful ordered-history intertwiner through a singular source domain.
Proved [L, terminal oscillation input]: for every real C and natural threshold M, some N≥M, N≥3 satisfies `psiRemainder N < -C*sqrt(N)`, unconditionally. The actual compact-test source average plus its damped spectral response obeys a one-sided damping law under RH; the archimedean density pays the full polar forcing. Reflection, recurrence, and a centered positive Fourier pairing force response escape. A contrary one-sided Chebyshev bound itself implies RH, removing that premise from the final oscillation theorem. The ratio-one compensation square and signed history floor remain unpaid.
Proved [L, full ratio-one compensation]: for every real cap B and threshold M, some K≥M has `sqrt(K+2)+canonicalShift(K)>B`, unconditionally. A hypothetical cap bounds the actual Abel primitive quadratically; its all-points right-derivative comparison forces a logarithmic mass lower error. A positive-measure Landau argument then forces RH internally, and the existing RH mass estimate plus negative Chebyshev escape contradicts the cap with the full square and centroid compensation retained. Every fixed additive terminal cap is therefore excluded. The complete signed-history floor remains open.
Proved [L, full parabolic source fiber]: at one fixed prime, three moving-depth stencils independently retain physical mass, centroid, and half-second moment while their complete Xi jets tend to zero. The primitive jet/moment graph closure contains every vertical vector in C^3. The centroid stencil retains a strictly negative complete parabolic form, ruling out every lower budget by a constant times Xi norm squared for this finite signed source family. The earlier second-moment vertical vector was null and did not establish this energy obstruction. This does not bound or falsify the native positive prime-head matched-minus-quantile history.
Proved [L, complete paired source current]: the absolute critical-mass discrepancy and literal matched-to-quantile displacement escape every fixed bound on active prime-power tails. The exact mass-normalized paired cross current also escapes. Two literal signed primitive sources, retaining both matched and quantile moment sums with identical source coefficients, have complete paired current exactly minus one at arbitrarily small full Xi norm, with the moving node arbitrarily late. Thus no Xi-norm-square lower budget exists even after quantile subtraction. The positive survivor is an explicit nonvanishing complete paired current at vanishing Xi norm. This still neither bounds nor falsifies the native positive-head linear floor.
Proved [L, native positive-head source current]: the obstruction persists for the complete native prime heads, retaining every original relative coefficient. Cofinal negative linear history excursions and the native mass bound produce a common positive scaling with complete paired form exactly minus one at arbitrarily small full Xi norm. No constant Xi-norm-square lower bound holds even eventually for the unscaled native heads. The positive survivor uses the literal native family itself; the arithmetic linear floor remains open.
Proved [L, local signed divisor payment]: complete centered-divisor rows with the literal logarithmic forcing coefficient are positive at two-prime square births, arbitrarily late. Their exact birth coefficient is a reciprocal Euler logarithmic jet. Summing all rows over the divisors of one modulus at an aligned time collapses the Möbius-log coefficients to the endpoint and von Mangoldt weights. The complete packet is nonpositive at its square birth, with every original quotient label active. Arbitrary residue phases, moving clipped packets, and the half-order history assembly remain uncontrolled; neither native square debt is thereby paid.
Proved [L, full-period sign obstruction]: the aligned birth sign does not extend to nonpositive full-period payment. For every fixed pair of distinct primes at least three, the actual complete packet has strictly positive logarithmic half-order response on every sufficiently late aligned period, with all labels active. Its exact all-phase decomposition retains both logarithmic channels; the normalized response tends to an explicit positive primitive sum. The positive survivor is this exact signed time coefficient and cofinally late actual packets. Bounded positive allowances, interactions between packets, and the complete native linear floor remain open.
Proved [L, complete-region critical deletion]: the entire unweighted Möbius centered-divisor region with product label d*j<=sqrt(k) has half-order action bounded by R*(1+log R), R=floor(sqrt(M-1)), and hence vanishes after division by M. Exact finite reindexing retains every active label and endpoint. The native Möbius Abel moment equals twice this complete-region action plus the joint smooth remainder and active complementary labels d*j>sqrt(k). RH is exactly uniform critical boundedness of that joint remainder. The complete region is now paid; the complementary signed assembly and both native square debts remain open.
Proved [L, delayed repayment head barrier]: the actual finite-core head has a positive inverse-five-halves moment equal to its unit-source moment times product(1-1/p). Agreement below X and critical linear tail envelopes C*x,D*x force moment difference at most 2*(C+D)/sqrt(X). Every sufficiently fresh helper family with fixed positive reciprocal-prime mass therefore forces a later retained-head square spike beyond any prescribed K*M^2 budget. Actual inherited omitted-prime debts require that mass for late net half repayment, so unrestricted delayed repair cannot retain one all-later-cutoff critical head budget. This controls literal head values, not merely a sufficient upper-bound constant. Timely joint movement of the core and cutoff remains open; neither native square debt is paid and RH is not falsified.

This does not decide a discrete prime-grid pair inequality or pay the complete signed source.
Proved [L, actual prime-grid minors]: at M=N*a*b, the unsigned four-channel minor divided by M tends jointly to c*(U(N)/sqrt(N)-c), where c is the exact unsigned mean. The top product retains its finite quotient N. The same cofinally large distinct primes p,q give a positive signed-channel minor at 12*p*q and a negative one at 2*p*q, each with a fixed linear margin and all four product indices active. Möbius signs preserve the minor. Either uniform discrete-prime-grid sign fails; this is not a bound for the full signed pair sum or a positive full energy increment.
Proved [L, uniform source-profile power remainder]: the actual normalized unsigned channel satisfies |V_M(d)/sqrt(M)-c|<=C/floor(M/d)^(1/40), with one constant and one quotient threshold for every positive dilation. A growing reciprocal-phase Fourier cutoff pays the terminal Bernoulli residue; the reciprocal correction, exact square births, smooth term, and clipped endpoint are retained. Proved [L, literal hyperbolic Möbius coefficient]: sum_{d*e<=M} mu(d)*mu(e)=o(M), retaining every common-divisor pair. Proved [L, full hyperbolic signed-pair deletion]: the literal sum of A_M(d)*A_M(e) over all positive d,e<=M with d*e<=M is o(M^2). Exact quotient-cell homothety and discrete Abel summation pay each fixed prefix. Symmetry rewrites the variable-profile error into centered prefix rows, uniformly dominated by a summable e^(-41/40) envelope. Dominated convergence and the literal mu*mu limit pay the whole growing sector. The exact full-square partition retains every gcd class and both diagonal parts; the normalized full square differs from the complementary sum d*e>M by a term tending to zero. A uniform quadratic upper bound for that complementary sum and both native square debts remain open.
Proved [L, complete lcm-sector payment]: one unconditional constant bounds the absolute native signed sum over lcm(d,e)<=M by B*M^2. ### TH — Theta moment positivity (legacy CLG/TTF, K-RVM, S6F, TCD, UEB)
Target: RH ⇔ normalized prime–archimedean moments `m_n` form a positive moment sequence; the integer-polynomial trace Hankel on the degree-two tail alone suffices [L] (F0092); Bernstein negative mass bounded (F0152); theta tilt-flux Bezoutian PSD (F0212); Gaussian heat trace `≥ 0` (F0103). Also F0041, F0100, F0270, F0104, F0113, F0118, F0254.
Proved [L]: literal complete theta source `Φ` satisfies `(log Φ)''''(x)<0` for every real `x`, including the modular centre, by a three-atom margin and a bound on the complete derivative tail. ## Falsification routes

A disproof needs one certified value of the wrong sign. Lean-certified falsifier forms (endpoints §FAL and falsifier clauses of live families):
- Localized Hermite form `Re Σc_ic_j p_{i+j+2}(t)<0` at an off-spectral base point; high odd monomial at a zero's ordinate (F0125). Decides an isolated off-line zero by one derivative evaluation; the isolation hypothesis cannot be removed (MUST-432). E: R:LocalHermitePositivity; R:TraceFormSignature.
- Negative shifted reciprocal moment or Hankel quadratic (F0260); negative completed squared-ξ source jet (F0196); negative Gaussian heat trace, Turán minor or heat-Hankel minor (F0103).
- One real `t` with `Ξ'(t)²<Ξ(t)Ξ''(t)` (F0240); positive completed-flux quadratic coefficient on the line (F0047).
- One multiple zero of de Bruijn H_t at positive time refutes RH [L]; negative-time collisions do not. E: R:DeBruijnNewmanCollisionCriterion.
- One finite certified prime-side Pick witness at a positive shift (F0257); one finite continued Cauchy packet with negative source energy (F0274).
- Rouché sphere domination planting a zero in `Re s>½` (F0150); an odd-order right-half zero (F0190); integral faithful Arakelov coordinates (F0089).
- Violating any of 63 standalone consequences of RH (F0276), e.g. `ψ(N)−N=O(√N log²N)`, `Re ξ'/ξ>0` on `Re s>½`, Li positivity, lag bound `|Φ| ≤ Φ(0)`.
Limits: numerical PSD checks never outrun the verified zero-free height (MUST-245); conditional level-`S` Hodge positivity excludes fixed-depth zeros up to `T≈exp(c_δ S)` but does not supply that positivity or exclude arbitrarily shallow zeros (MUST-475–477); paired Li coefficients are never eventually negative, only both-signed infinitely often (CANNOT-194r).
